## Module 2 — Top-level admin panel (`docs/project-documentation/modules/module-2-admin-panel.md`)

Planned 2026-11-18 (M2-PLAN). UI spec: `docs/design/admin.md`. Order: backend before the screen that needs it, all `admin/` rows before the single `client/` row (M2-T13). Admin routes are TanStack Router file routes under `src/routes/_authed/admin/*`; every API call goes through TanStack Query at same-origin `/api/*`. New backend routes sit in `AdminModule` behind `SuperAdminGuard` (impersonated sessions → 403), with e2e in the M1-T2 style (`createAuthActors`, cleanup leaves the test DB empty). Rows marked **needs decision** need a product answer from the user before (or at the start of) that session; don't guess.

|---|---|---|---|

(boolean, default false) }` → 201 `{ message, ticket }`. `PATCH :id/status` `{ status }` → 200 `{ ticket, changed }`. 404 `Ticket not found` for unknown/malformed ids. Regression tests: unit (every key, 18 malformed tickets values, dashboard range/from/to) + component (`-tickets` 6 malformed URLs → only `skip=0&take=20`, one query key, no thread fetch, default filters/empty pane; `-clients` 3 URLs; `-dashboard` 3 URLs; `-login` non-string redirect), all red without the fix. **Flakes:** root cause = `autoCodeSplitting`: each route component is a lazy import Vite transforms on first render, so every file's first render (shell/login→dashboard/dashboard/system) took 0.4–0.6 s idle and > 1 s under load, past the 1 s `findBy*` default. Fix: `tanstackRouter({ autoCodeSplitting: !process.env.VITEST })` in `vite.config.ts` (components load eagerly at file import time, which has no timeout); dev/prod still split. No timeout raised, no retries. |

A first reply that finds the ticket IN_PROGRESS/RESOLVED changes nothing. `ticket.replied` + response shape unchanged. ## Module 3 — Client Auth, Impersonation Handoff & Meta (FB/IG) OAuth Connection (`docs/project-documentation/modules/module-3-client-meta.md`)

Planned 2026-11-18 (M3-PLAN). UI spec: `docs/design/client.md` (§1 language, §5 banner) + `docs/project-documentation/screens-inventory.md` C2/C3/C6. Order: backend before the screen that needs it (M3-T1…T7 backend, M3-T8…T11 `client/`). Builds on: Better Auth workspaces (`organization` plugin, sign-up creates the workspace), `WorkspaceAccessGuard` + our `@Roles` + `@CurrentWorkspace()` (M1-T1, pattern in `src/workspaces/workspaces.controller.ts`), `RedisModule`, env validation `src/config/env.validation.ts` (optional modules disabled when env unset, like `StorageModule`), client `_app` layout (`ssr: 'data-only'` guard, placeholder top bar with the EN/BN toggle), `useLanguage()`/`content(en, bn)`, `sessionQueryOptions`, and `/impersonate` (done in M2-T13). Conventions: Drizzle schema in `backend/src/db/schema/` (`id()`/`timestamps`, plural snake_case tables), `drizzle-kit generate` then review the SQL (never `push`); every query scoped by `workspace_id`; Graph API calls only through one mockable client (no live Meta calls in unit/e2e); e2e in the M1-T2 style (`createAuthActors`, cleanup leaves the test DB empty except the 4 `plans` rows); client: TanStack Query for every call at same-origin `/api/*`, shadcn OKLCH tokens, `rounded-full` buttons/pills/inputs, component tests mount the real file route under a bare root (M2-T13). Rows marked **needs user decision** must not start until the user answers; don't guess.

**Decisions (all answered 2026-11-18; each names the rows it affects):**
- **D1 Meta app credentials — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M3-T3):** env `META_APP_ID`, `META_APP_SECRET`, `META_LOGIN_CONFIG_ID`, `META_OAUTH_REDIRECT_URI` (full callback URL, host per D6; all four together, client disabled when unset) · `appsecret_proof` on every token call + "Require App Secret" switched on in the app's Advanced settings · Meta **test app** (child of the prod app) for local + staging, live app for production; secrets only in git-ignored `backend/.env` / `backend/.env.staging` and the prod host's secret store. Still open (not blocking code): app owner/Business portfolio, App Review + Business Verification timeline (needed for the live check in GATE).
- **D2 Token encryption at rest — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M3-T1):** env key `TOKEN_ENCRYPTION_KEY` (base64 of 32 bytes) · versioned key id + keyring (`TOKEN_ENCRYPTION_KEY_ID`, decrypt-only `TOKEN_ENCRYPTION_PREVIOUS_KEYS`) · one `text` column `v1:<keyId>:<iv>:<tag>:<ciphertext>` · AAD = workspace + purpose (+ subject id).
- **D3 Webhooks + local tunnelling — ANSWERED 2026-11-18:** all webhook work is **Module 4**: M3-T7 moved to Module 4 (with D8's Deauthorize/Data Deletion); the M3-T6 toggle only flips `is_active` (`subscribed_apps` in Module 4). Defaults for Module 4: verify token from env, `X-Hub-Signature-256` checked in a route guard on the raw body, cloudflared tunnel.
- **D4 Permissions, login product, IG scope — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M3-T2):** Facebook Login for Business (user-token `config_id` from env) · scopes `pages_show_list`, `pages_read_engagement`, `pages_manage_engagement`, `pages_read_user_content`, `pages_manage_metadata`, `business_management`, `instagram_basic`, `instagram_manage_comments` · IG via the Page's `instagram_business_account` (no Instagram Login) · exchange to the 60-day long-lived user token → non-expiring Page tokens · launch FB + IG · extra columns `social_pages.ig_username`, `meta_connections.granted_scopes text[]`.
- **D5 API shape, roles, impersonation — ANSWERED 2026-11-18:** routes `/api/workspaces/:workspaceId/meta/oauth/url` + `/api/workspaces/:workspaceId/pages…` with `WorkspaceAccessGuard` + our `@Roles('owner','admin')` (member → 403) · impersonated sessions: **connect blocked (403)** so an admin's own Facebook account is never linked to a client, **page toggle allowed**. Only the OAuth callback lives outside the workspace prefix (`/api/meta/oauth/callback`, D6).
- **D6 OAuth redirect + state — ANSWERED 2026-11-18:** redirect URI on the **client origin** `app.<domain>/api/meta/oauth/callback` via the same-origin proxy (session cookie rides along; the callback checks the state belongs to the same user + workspace) · state = random key in **Redis, 10-min TTL, single-use (GETDEL)** · callback lands back on the page that started it (`/onboarding` or `/pages`, allowlisted, stored in the state) with `?meta=connected` or `?meta=error&reason=…`.
- **D7 Connection cardinality — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M3-T2):** one `meta_connections` row per workspace (unique `workspace_id`; re-connect updates it in place) · `social_pages.page_id` globally unique (+ `ig_business_id`): a Page already in another workspace is skipped with a reason, so each webhook maps to one workspace · Pages missing on re-connect: keep the row, `is_active = false`, Page token cleared · disconnect endpoint later (not Module 3).
- **D8 Token expiry/revocation — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M3-T2):** `meta_connections.status` ACTIVE|NEEDS_REAUTH|REVOKED + `status_changed_at`; Graph error 190 → NEEDS_REAUTH (UI shows Reconnect) · `debug_token` job in Module 4 (BullMQ) · Deauthorize + Data Deletion callbacks in Module 4 with the webhook work (before App Review), so M3-T7 keeps only what D3 puts in Module 3.
- **D9 Graph API version — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M3-T3):** pin `v26.0` (latest, released 2026-07-29) as `DEFAULT_GRAPH_API_VERSION` in `src/meta/meta-graph.config.ts`, optional env override `META_GRAPH_API_VERSION` (`^v\d+\.\d$`) · bump = change the constant, read the changelog for Pages/IG comment changes, run tests.
- **D10 Plan limit `connectedPages` — ANSWERED 2026-11-18:** enforce **now** from `plans.feature_config.limits.connectedPages` (via `normalizeFeatureConfig`); a workspace with no plan gets the **Free** plan's limit · sync stores **every Page inactive** (user switches them on) · switching on past the limit is refused with **409** carrying the limit + current active count (UI shows an upgrade message).
- **D11 Impersonation banner — ANSWERED 2026-11-18:** build it **now** (M3-T9) in the `_app` layout when `session.session.impersonatedBy` is set (en + bn); **Exit = sign out** the impersonated session, clear queries, then show "You can close this tab". Gate item 3 stays as written.
- **D12 Gate E2E harness — ANSWERED 2026-11-18:** **committed Playwright suite** (`@playwright/test` dev dependency, own folder/project; the admin → client impersonation flow is its first spec), built in M3-T12. User also asked: install **`playwright-cli`** in the Docker image used to run it, and install its agent skill into this project folder with `playwright-cli install --skills`.
- **D13 Register + onboarding product rules — ANSWERED 2026-11-18:** register fields = name, email, password + **business name** (used as the workspace name) · email verification + password reset **later** (no email sender yet) · users go to `/onboarding` after sign-up **and whenever the workspace has 0 active Pages**; it has a "Skip for now" link to `/dashboard`. · **0-Pages redirect: entry points only, answered 2026-11-18:** only after sign-in / sign-up without an explicit `?redirect=` (active workspace 0 active Pages → `/onboarding`, else `/dashboard`); `/dashboard` itself never redirects (so "Skip for now" needs no stored flag), `/impersonate` keeps landing on `/dashboard`, a failed pages check (network/5xx/403) → `/dashboard`.
- **D14 C6 boosted posts — ANSWERED 2026-11-18:** **Module 6**. Module 3 `/pages` = base view only (list, status, toggle, reconnect, limit + needs-reauth messages); no `ads_read`, no Marketing API.
- **Implementation choices M3-T4…T6 — CONFIRMED by user 2026-11-18:** M3-T4: OAuth callback without a session → `?meta=error&reason=unauthenticated` (not straight to login); connect URL for a workspace that isn't the session's active one → 409; callback re-checks owner/admin + not impersonating. M3-T5: Pages skipped because they belong to another workspace are reported only as `?meta=connected&skipped=N` + a server log line; a Page whose IG account belongs to another workspace is left untouched; a Page missing on re-connect keeps its `page_id` (no other workspace can claim it until a disconnect endpoint exists, later module). M3-T6: pages list = `{ items, pageLimit: { limit, activeCount } }`; 409 `PAGE_LIMIT_REACHED` (`details.limit/activeCount`) + `PAGE_TOKEN_MISSING`; `AllExceptionsFilter` passes `code` + `details` through on 4xx (convention for all error bodies); Free plan limit = 1 Page, missing/malformed plan row → limit 0 (fail closed).

## Module 4 — Meta Webhook Ingestion & BullMQ Async Queue (`docs/project-documentation/modules/module-4-webhooks-queue.md`)

Planned 2026-11-18 (M4-PLAN). Spec goal: ingest live FB Page + IG comment webhooks with HMAC verification, a 200 OK in < 200 ms and idempotent BullMQ queuing; the worker de-duplicates by `comment_id` and stores `comments` rows as `PENDING` (Module 5 moderates them). Also carries Module 3's leftovers: M3-T7 (webhook verify + signature, Deauthorize + Data Deletion, tunnel; D3/D8), the `debug_token` job (D8), connection status + disconnect + `subscribed_apps` (D3/D7) and the two M3-GATE findings. Builds on: `MetaGraphClient` (M3-T3, the only code that calls Meta; extend it, e2e fakes it), `meta_connections`/`social_pages` (M3-T2, `page_id`/`ig_business_id` globally unique = one workspace per webhook), `META_APP_SECRET` env (M3-T3), Pages API + toggle tx (M3-T6, `src/meta/social-pages.*`), `RedisModule` (`REDIS_URL`), `AdminSystemService.queues()` stub (M2-T10; admin `QueuesCard` in `src/routes/_authed/admin/system.tsx` already renders `QueueStats[]`), client `/pages` + `components/meta/` + `lib/meta-pages.ts` (M3-T10/T11). Note: `main.ts` / `createNestApplication` use `bodyParser: false` (Better Auth), so the raw body for the HMAC check must be captured per route. Conventions as in Module 3: Drizzle schema in `backend/src/db/schema/` (`id()`/`timestamps`, plural snake_case tables, so the spec's `WebhookEvent`/`Comment` become `webhook_events`/`comments`), `drizzle-kit generate` then review the SQL (never `push`); every tenant query scoped by `workspace_id`; no live Meta calls in unit/e2e; e2e in the M1-T2 style (`createAuthActors`, cleanup leaves the test DB empty except the 4 `plans` rows; e2e Redis is stubbed, so queues need a test double or a real test Redis, see D3); errors may carry `code` + `details` (4xx); client: TanStack Query at same-origin `/api/*`, `content(en, bn)`, shadcn OKLCH tokens, `rounded-full` buttons/pills/inputs. All decisions answered 2026-11-18 (below); anything new that is a product call → ask, don't guess.

**Decisions (all answered 2026-11-18; each names the rows it affects):**
- **D1 Webhook routes + public host — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T3, M4-T4, M4-T13):** `GET` + `POST /api/webhooks/meta` (spec path under the global prefix) · Meta calls the **backend host directly** (not the client proxy; raw body untouched; locally via the D5 tunnel → `localhost:3000`) · missing/invalid `X-Hub-Signature-256` → **401** · verify token env `META_WEBHOOK_VERIFY_TOKEN` (optional like the other `META_*`; unset → 503).
- **D2 Webhook fields/topics — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T4, M4-T5, M4-T8):** Page `feed` (`item: comment`) + IG `comments`, **all verbs**: `add` (new row), `edited` (update message; scammers edit a bKash number in later), `remove` (mark deleted, never hide it later) · skip comments written by the Page/IG account itself · no `live_comments`/`mentions` · `subscribed_fields=feed` on `subscribed_apps`.
- **D3 Queues — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T1, M4-T4, M4-T5, M4-T7):** three queues `comment-ingest` (webhook → `comments` row), `comment-moderation` (Module 5; register now, no worker logic yet), `meta-token-check` (scheduled jobs) · 5 attempts, exponential backoff 2 s · `jobId` = `<platform>:<commentId>:<verb>` · `removeOnComplete` 1 h / 1000, `removeOnFail` 7 d / 1000 (Redis 30 MB) · workers in the API process for now · `@nestjs/bullmq` if it supports NestJS 12, else plain `bullmq` · e2e uses a fake queue provider (like the OAuth state store).
- **D4 Storage + routing — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T2, M4-T5, M4-T7):** `webhook_events.event_id` = `<platform>:<commentId>:<verb>` · comments for unknown / inactive Pages or REVOKED connections are **dropped** with a log line · `comments.status` only `PENDING` now (Module 5 adds values) · **only `raw_payload` is cleared after 30 days** (daily purge job; comment rows with text/author/status stay) · **lifetime analytics kept forever:** a daily aggregate table (per workspace + Page + platform + Dhaka day: received/edited/removed counts, Module 5 adds blocked etc.) updated by the worker in the same tx, never purged, no personal data · quota counting in Module 5/8.
- **D5 Local tunnel — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T13):** **zrok2** (installed in WSL + Windows, v2.0.8, account enabled) with the **static name `shield-comment-dev`** in the `public` namespace → `https://shield-comment-dev.shares.zrok.io` (name not reserved yet: the script creates it if missing). Repo script (e.g. `scripts/tunnel.sh`) remembers the name and runs `zrok2 share public localhost:3000 -n public:shield-comment-dev` (target port overridable), prints the webhook URL; agents may start/stop it when needed.
- **D6 Deauthorize + Data Deletion — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T6, M4-T12):** `POST /api/meta/deauthorize` + `POST /api/meta/data-deletion` (`@Public()`, `signed_request` verified) · Deauthorize: status REVOKED + all Pages switched off + tokens cleared · Data Deletion: queued job deletes the connection, its Pages and their comments (daily aggregates stay, no personal data); random confirmation code stored in `meta_data_deletion_requests`; status page on the **client** `app.<domain>/data-deletion?code=` (en/bn).
- **D7 Disconnect — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T9, M4-T11):** `DELETE /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/meta/connection`, owner/admin, **blocked while impersonated** (403, like connect) · hard delete `meta_connections` + `social_pages` rows (frees `page_id`/`ig_business_id` claims) · comments kept with their Page link set to null · best-effort `DELETE /{page-id}/subscribed_apps` per Page + `DELETE /me/permissions` (failures logged, never block). **Exception — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (user, M4-T9 choice 4, "Keep the exception (Recommended)"):** the app permissions are revoked only when the Facebook user has no other workspace still connected; the other workspace keeps working (built in M4-T9). The client dialog says the permission is revoked unless another ShieldComment workspace still uses that Facebook account (M4-T11).
- **D8 `debug_token` job — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T7):** daily at **03:00 Asia/Dhaka** · checks ACTIVE + NEEDS_REAUTH rows (a fixed token goes back to ACTIVE) · user token only · `is_valid=false` or a required D4-scope missing → NEEDS_REAUTH; REVOKED only from Deauthorize · no expiry warning yet (no email sender).
- **D9 Connection status + NEEDS_REAUTH — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T9, M4-T11):** `GET …/pages` adds `connection: { status, statusChangedAt, tokenExpiresAt } | null` · **soft block:** NEEDS_REAUTH → switching off allowed, switching on 409 `CONNECTION_NEEDS_REAUTH`, webhooks still ingested; REVOKED → no ingestion · Reconnect banner on `/pages` now (dashboard banner in Module 6).
- **D10 `subscribed_apps` on toggle — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T8):** Graph subscribe **before** the DB tx; failure → **502 `META_SUBSCRIBE_FAILED`**, nothing changes (UI rolls back); `token_invalid` → connection NEEDS_REAUTH · unsubscribe on switch-off is best effort (always switches off) · one-off backfill script for Pages already active from Module 3.
- **D11 Rate-limit IP fix — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T10):** **both**: the e2e seed / internal callers send `X-Forwarded-For`, and the backend falls back to the socket address when the header is missing, so nothing lands in `auth:no-trusted-ip|…`.
- **D12 Meta app owner — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T13):** a new **ShieldComment Business portfolio** owns the prod app + test app (user sets it up). **Skip the live Meta check for now**: build and gate with fakes only. **M4-T13 scope (user, answered 2026-11-19):** "Script + docs only (Recommended)": tunnel script + setup docs + live-smoke checklist; the live Meta smoke stays skipped until the test app exists (agents may create the zrok2 name and start/stop the share briefly).
- **D13 App Review + Business Verification — ANSWERED 2026-11-18:** start **Business Verification now** (user, outside the code); submit **App Review after Module 5** (screencast can show hiding a scam comment; Data Deletion URL from M4-T6 + privacy policy needed).
- **Gate finding (a) — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T11):** impersonated `/pages` empty state hides the Connect CTA and shows the same impersonation notice as `/onboarding` (en/bn).
- **Repeated `edited` dedupe — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T4, M4-T5; from M4-T2 open a):** "Add payload hash (Recommended)": an `edited` job id / `event_id` carries a short hash of the new message, so exact retries are still ignored and every different edit is stored; `add`/`remove` keep the D3/D4 format. Built as `<platform>:<commentId>:edited-<16 hex>` (`commentEditHash()` = sha256 of the message, first 16 hex; still 3 colon parts for BullMQ), DB check relaxed in migration `0012_webhook_event_edit_hash`.
- **Out-of-order `edited`/`remove` — ANSWERED 2026-11-18 (M4-T5 pre-check, user):** "Edit inserts, remove marks (Recommended)": `edited` with no comment row creates the comment from the payload as `PENDING` (a scam edit still gets moderated); `remove` with no row stores a deleted placeholder (no text), so a late `add` can't bring it back; counts move as for a normal edit/remove. A late `add` never un-deletes or overwrites (no-op).
- **Orphaned comments vs Data Deletion — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M4-T9; from M4-T6 open b, user via orchestrator):** "Keep FB user id on comments (Recommended)": comments keep D7's behaviour (kept on disconnect), but disconnect stamps each orphaned comment with the connection's `facebook_user_id`, so a later Data Deletion still finds and deletes them (+ their `webhook_events`). Daily counts stay. Built as nullable `comments.facebook_user_id` (numeric check, partial index, migration `0014_comments_facebook_user_id`).
- **Orchestration scope (user, 2026-11-18):** run M4-T1…M4-T12 then M4-GATE; M4-T13 (zrok script + live smoke) in its own session later.

IG: `feed` only (D2): IG `comments` come from the app's `instagram` webhook object, Meta only needs the app installed on the linked Page with any Page field (from Meta's docs, no live call). **Toggle** (`SocialPagesService.toggle`): **on** = pre-checks without locks or Graph (Page of this workspace else 404 · already on → no-op `changed:false`, **no Graph call** · no token → 409 `PAGE_TOKEN_MISSING` · limit → 409 `PAGE_LIMIT_REACHED`) → Meta or cipher unset → **503** (no Graph call) → decrypt (fails → 409 `PAGE_TOKEN_MISSING`, i.e. reconnect) → subscribe outside any tx → Graph failure → **502 `code: META_SUBSCRIBE_FAILED`**, nothing written (`token_invalid` also marks the connection) → the M3-T6 tx re-checks under locks; if it refuses (4xx, e.g. limit raced / Page gone) → best-effort unsubscribe, then the 4xx. **Off** = tx first (always), then `unsubscribeBestEffort` after commit (awaited, never throws; re-reads the Page scoped and skips it if gone / on again / no token / Meta unset; failures logged with ids + kind only, response unchanged). `PageSubscriptionService` (`src/meta/page-subscription.service.ts`) does decrypt + Graph + NEEDS_REAUTH for toggle + backfill; token queries in `page-subscription.queries.ts` (`findWorkspacePageToken`, `listActivePagesWithToken`, `markConnectionNeedsReauth` = one UPDATE: `ACTIVE` only (never over REVOKED, `status_changed_at` only on change) and only while the Page still holds the token that failed (re-connect wins)). `token_invalid` on unsubscribe also marks NEEDS_REAUTH. `AllExceptionsFilter`: 5xx keep `code` only if in `PUBLIC_SERVER_ERROR_CODES` (`META_SUBSCRIBE_FAILED`); `details` stay 4xx-only. **Backfill:** `backfillPageSubscriptions()` (`src/meta/page-subscription.backfill.ts`) + script `src/scripts/subscribe-active-pages.ts`: every active Page with a token (system-wide keyset read; NEEDS_REAUTH write scoped by `workspace_id` + Page row id), sequential, 200 ms apart, rate limit stops the run (rest `notAttempted`), prints counts only; Meta/cipher unset → "Skipped …" exit 0; exit 1 if any failed/undecryptable/not attempted. Run: host `cd backend && npm run build && npm run meta:subscribe-pages` · compose `docker compose exec backend node dist/scripts/subscribe-active-pages.js`. Idempotent (re-run any time). **Client:** `pageToggleFailure` → `{ kind: 'subscribe-failed' }` for 502 `META_SUBSCRIBE_FAILED` (en + bn message in `pageToggleErrorMessage`; optimistic rollback unchanged). Known small race (accepted): an unsubscribe after switch-off/refusal can land after a concurrent re-activation's subscribe; re-running the backfill repairs it. Tests: unit +45 (client calls, service, backfill, toggle order/502/503/compensation, filter); e2e +12 (`meta-page-subscribe.e2e-spec.ts`; `workspace-pages.e2e-spec.ts` now uses the strict fake `test/utils/fake-meta-graph.ts`); client +2 (502 rollback en + bn). |

## Module 5 — Bangla/Banglish AI Classifier & Meta Action Executor (`docs/project-documentation/modules/module-5-ai-moderation.md`)

Planned 2026-11-19 (M5-PLAN), decisions answered + rows re-planned 2026-11-19. Spec goal: classify every new or edited comment (Bangla script, Banglish, English, mixed) into `SAFE_INQUIRY` / `COMPETITOR_POACH` / `SCAM_PHISHING` / `SPAM_IRRELEVANT` / `ABUSIVE_TOXIC` with a structured LLM output `{ category, confidence_score, reasoning }`, then hide / delete / reply through the Meta Graph API per workspace policy. Gate: golden suite of 30+ real-world comments ≥ 90 % accuracy + mocked Graph tests for the exact hide/delete/reply payloads. Builds on: `AiGatewayService` (`backend/src/ai-gateway/ai-gateway.service.ts`: plain `fetch` to the OpenAI-compatible `/v1/chat/completions`, default model `gpt-oss-20b`, optional env `NEON_AI_GATEWAY_BASE_URL`/`NEON_AI_GATEWAY_TOKEN`/`AI_GATEWAY_MODEL`, `AiGatewayError{status, reason}`; no JSON mode, error kinds or retries yet; **live calls answer 403 `paid_plan_required` until AI credits are bought**), `QUEUE_NAMES.commentModeration` (`src/queue/queue.constants.ts`: registered, no worker; `DEFAULT_JOB_OPTIONS` 5 attempts exp 2 s; `commentJobId()` 3-part id rule; `withTimeout()` around queue calls), the hand-off `CommentIngestProcessor.handOffToModeration()` (`src/comments/comment-ingest.processor.ts`: after commit, `moderate: true` for created comments and applied edits of non-deleted ones; enqueues nothing yet), `comments` + `daily_comment_stats` (`src/db/schema/comments.ts`: `COMMENT_STATUSES = ['PENDING']` + check constraint; queries in `src/comments/comments.queries.ts`), `MetaGraphClient` (`src/meta/meta-graph.client.ts`: private `request(method, path, params, secrets)` = form body for POST, query for GET/DELETE, `appsecret_proof`; `MetaGraphError.kind` incl. `token_invalid`/`rate_limited`/`permission` + `retryable`, `src/meta/meta-graph.errors.ts`), the Page-token pattern of `PageSubscriptionService` + `markConnectionNeedsReauth` (`src/meta/page-subscription.*`: decrypt with `TokenCipherService` context `meta_page_token`, Graph outside the tx, `token_invalid` → NEEDS_REAUTH), granted scopes `pages_manage_engagement` + `instagram_manage_comments` (`META_LOGIN_SCOPES`), plans `feature_config` (`src/db/schema/plans.ts`, read via `normalizeFeatureConfig` in `src/admin/plan-rules.ts`: `limits.monthlyComments` free 500 / starter 2,500 / growth 15,000 / pro 75,000, which becomes `limits.monthlyAiCredits` in M5-T11 (D15); flags `banglaAiFilter`, `autoHideScams` (all plans), `autoReplyTemplates` (growth/pro), `customBlocklist`) + `workspaces.plan_id` (null = free plan, Module 3 D10 rule), admin stubs waiting for data (`AdminSystemService` for A6 "AI latency & token cost", dashboard `comments {scanned, blocked}` = 0, A3 Quota bar placeholder), scheduled-job pattern `MetaJobSchedulers` (`src/meta-callbacks/meta-scheduled.jobs.ts`), e2e doubles `test/utils/fake-queue.ts`, `fake-worker.ts` (processors tested via `app.get(X).process(job)`), `fake-meta-graph.ts` (strict: unexpected calls throw). Own comments/replies come back as webhooks but are already dropped by the envelope parser (`from.id === entry.id`, M4-T4). **Kept out (Module 6):** `ModerationRule` keywords/URL/BD-phone static rule engine that runs before the AI, `/comments` feed + manual override endpoint/UI (C5), `/rules` (C7), `/playground` (C8), dashboard/analytics (C4/C9). **AI credits (D15) are the only plan limit for moderation** (cost per AI call by the D8 context level); **the AI provider, model and keys are chosen in `/admin/settings`** (D16; keys encrypted in the DB, `.env` fallback). **One `client/` row in Module 5:** the D8 "AI analysis" card (M5-T15); all other client moderation screens are Module 6 (D5/D10). Conventions as in Module 4: Drizzle schema in `backend/src/db/schema/`, `drizzle-kit generate` then review the SQL (never `push`); every tenant query scoped by `workspace_id`; **no live LLM or Meta calls in unit/e2e** (strict fakes that throw on unexpected calls; live checks are opt-in scripts); comment text is untrusted input (never logged, never followed as instructions; logs = ids/kinds/counts only); errors may carry `code` + `details` (4xx); admin: TanStack Query, shadcn OKLCH tokens. All decisions below were answered 2026-11-19; anything new that is a product call → ask, don't guess. Live AI rows (M5-T16) wait for the user's Neon AI credits or a provider key; M5-T17 waits for the Meta test app.

**Decisions (all answered 2026-11-19; each names the rows it affects):**
- **D1 LLM provider, model + who pays — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T1, M5-T2, M5-T3, M5-T4, M5-T6, M5-T16):** **both** the Neon AI Gateway (with a model bake-off, M5-T16) **and** direct provider keys (OpenAI / Anthropic / Google); the active provider + model is chosen by the super admin in `/admin/settings` (D16) · ShieldComment pays (cost inside the plan prices; workspace use limited by D15 credits) · no live call until the user provides Neon AI credits or a provider key (live rows stop with `needs-decision`).
- **D2 AI failure behaviour — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T1, M5-T12):** fail open + sweeper: the comment stays visible with status `FAILED` + reason · transient errors (429, 5xx, timeout, network, one invalid JSON) use the D3 BullMQ retries · `not_configured` / `payment_required` / `auth` are not retried · a scheduled sweeper re-queues `PENDING`/`FAILED` comments younger than 24 h.
- **D3 Default action per category + mode — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T7, M5-T8, M5-T12):** auto mode on from the first comment, hide only: `SCAM_PHISHING`, `COMPETITOR_POACH`, `ABUSIVE_TOXIC`, `SPAM_IRRELEVANT` → hide; `SAFE_INQUIRY` → nothing · delete is never a default (a workspace may opt in per category through the D5 API).
- **D4 Confidence threshold — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T7, M5-T8, M5-T12):** one per-workspace threshold, default **0.80** (Module 6's `auto_hide_threshold` reuses it): ≥ threshold → the D3 action; below → no action, status `NEEDS_REVIEW` (visible; Module 6's feed lists it).
- **D5 Policy storage + where it is edited — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T7, M5-T8, M5-T15):** `moderation_policies` (one row per workspace, created lazily with the D3/D4/D6/D8 defaults: mode, action per category, threshold, auto-reply switches + en/bn texts, AI context level) + `GET/PATCH /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/moderation/policy` (owner/admin, impersonation rule like the Page toggle) · Module 6 builds `/rules` (C7) on it · Module 5's only client UI = the D8 "AI analysis" card (M5-T15). · **User wish (2026-11-19, golden review of abuse-01):** a client page listing each comment category with a description + an example comment, where the client picks Show / Hide / Delete per category; kept in **Module 6** (`/rules`, C7) on this API.
- **D6 Auto-reply — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T5, M5-T7, M5-T8, M5-T9, M5-T12):** off by default · only on plans with `autoReplyTemplates` (growth/pro) · per-category opt-in with one en + one bn text · the classifier returns `language` (`bn`/`banglish`/`en`) → bn text for bn/banglish, en otherwise · at most one auto-reply per comment, none to deleted comments · `SAFE_INQUIRY` only when the owner switches it on.
- **D7 Credit use — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T11, M5-T12, M5-T13):** credits (D15) are used on **every AI call**: the first classification and every re-classified edit · reserved atomically before the call (the balance never goes below 0; cost by the D8 level) · out of credits → no AI call, status `SKIPPED` reason `credits_exhausted`, comment stays visible, one warn line per workspace per day · no email yet (alerts = Module 7). Replaces the planned comment-count quota.
- **D8 Data sent to the LLM — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T5, M5-T7, M5-T8, M5-T10, M5-T11, M5-T12, M5-T15):** chosen **by the client per workspace** (owner/admin; stored in the D5 policy), 3 levels: **"Comment only"** (default, 1 credit: comment text trimmed ≤ 1,000 chars + platform + is-a-reply) · **"+ Page name"** (1 credit; better at competitor poaching) · **"+ post caption"** (2 credits; best context; caption from Graph, cached per post, M5-T10) · never the author name/id; phone numbers and links kept · the client "AI analysis" card shows a one-line pro/con per option in en + bn (user: more data sent = more credits but a better filter; comment-only analyses just the text).
- **D9 `reasoning` retention — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T5, M5-T7):** short English reasoning (≤ 280 chars) kept on the comment like its text (removed by Data Deletion with the comment) · raw LLM responses never stored.
- **D10 Human override scope — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T7, M5-T9):** Module 5 builds executor methods for hide / unhide / delete / reply + a `moderation_actions` log (actor = system or user id), no HTTP override endpoint · Module 6 adds `POST /comments/:id/override` + C5 · a human action stamps the comment so the AI never changes it again.
- **D11 Edited comments — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T8, M5-T9, M5-T12):** **re-check + auto-unhide** (the user's choice, not the recommended option): every applied edit is re-classified (and charged, D7); the AI may escalate (visible → hidden) and **un-hides a comment it hid itself when the edit is safe** · a human-stamped comment (D10) is never changed by the AI.
- **D12 Golden suite + live checks — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T6, M5-T16, M5-GATE):** agent-drafted fixture of ≥ 40 anonymised comments (Bangla script, Banglish, English, mixed; ≥ 6 per category; injection attempts, bKash/Nagad numbers, wa.me links, BD phone formats), **labels reviewed by the user** (M5-T6 pauses for it) · CI replays recorded responses (no paid calls) · accuracy = opt-in live `npm run ai:golden` (M5-T16) · gate (1) = that live run. **Label review done 2026-11-19:** all labels approved except emoji-only comments: positive/neutral reactions → `SAFE_INQUIRY`, negative/insulting → `ABUSIVE_TOXIC` (prompt `moderation-v2` teaches it); **if the M5-T16 live run shows the model can't tell emoji sentiment reliably, the fallback is to hide all emoji-only comments.**
- **D13 Which comments are moderated — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T12):** every stored comment with text on connected Pages, FB + IG, organic + ad posts, top-level + replies · skipped: deleted, no text, orphaned (`social_page_id` null), Page switched off · connection NEEDS_REAUTH → classified, Meta action skipped (`connection_needs_reauth`).
- **D14 Admin visibility — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T13, M5-T14):** A6 `/admin/system` "AI moderation" card (today + 7 d: classifications, tokens, estimated cost USD + BDT, avg/max latency, failures by kind; per provider + model) · A2 dashboard `comments {scanned, blocked}` from `daily_comment_stats` · A3 clients **credits** bar (`used / limit` credits this month) instead of the quota bar.
- **D15 Credits (new, replaces the comment-count limit) — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T11, M5-T12, M5-T13, M5-T14):** each plan gets N **AI credits per month** (`feature_config.limits.monthlyAiCredits`, seeded from today's `monthlyComments` numbers 500 / 2,500 / 15,000 / 75,000, editable in the `/admin/settings` plans editor, plan audit logged) · **credits are the only limit**: `limits.monthlyComments` stops being a limit; "up to ≈ N comments" is display text only (an estimate, not checked) · cost per AI call by the D8 level: comment only 1, + Page name 1, + post caption 2 · reset each Asia/Dhaka calendar month, no rollover · no plan = free plan · top-up packs later (Module 8).
- **D16 AI provider settings (new) — ANSWERED 2026-11-19 (M5-T1, M5-T2, M5-T3, M5-T4, M5-T6, M5-T16):** the super admin picks the active provider (**Neon AI gateway / OpenAI / Anthropic / Google**) + model in `/admin/settings` · API keys stored **encrypted in the DB** (`TokenCipherService`), `.env` values as fallback · keys never shown again after saving (masked) · "Test connection" button · every change in an audit log (keys only as set/cleared) · super admin only.
- **Module 6 carry-over (user, 2026-11-19):** `/rules` = per-category settings page: each category (`SAFE_INQUIRY` … `ABUSIVE_TOXIC`) shown with a short description + an example comment, and a Show / Hide / Delete choice per category (stored through the D5 policy API).
